Sampler candidates

Literature expectations, not measured PyAuto recommendations. Review a candidate and copy its investigation prompt.

dynesty

dynamic nested sampling · reviewed 2026-10-08

Paper ↗ · Official code ↗

Expected uses

  • Evidence and posterior baseline with adjustable effort allocation.

Limitations

  • Prior transforms and likelihood-contour exploration need validation; no universal dimensional scaling advantage.

Integration: integrated: DynestyStatic and DynestyDynamic

Benchmark evidence: not_assessed: this literature review establishes no benchmark result

Investigation prompt
Nautilus

neural-network importance nested sampling · reviewed 2026-10-08

Paper ↗ · Official code ↗

Expected uses

  • Evidence and posterior estimation for expensive gradient-free likelihoods.

Limitations

  • Learned proposal coverage and importance-weight stability need checking on unseen modes.

Integration: integrated: Nautilus

Benchmark evidence: not_assessed: this literature review establishes no benchmark result

Investigation prompt
BlackJAX NUTS

gradient Hamiltonian Monte Carlo · reviewed 2026-10-08

Paper ↗ · Official code ↗

Expected uses

  • Posterior refinement for differentiable JAX targets.

Limitations

  • Requires gradients, warmup and convergence diagnostics; NUTS alone supplies no evidence and can miss isolated modes.

Integration: integrated: BlackJAXNUTS

Benchmark evidence: not_assessed: this literature review establishes no benchmark result

Investigation prompt
UltraNest

reactive nested sampling · reviewed 2026-10-08

Paper ↗ · Official code ↗

Expected uses

  • Independent evidence cross-check for non-Gaussian and multimodal targets.

Limitations

  • Contour coverage and stopping settings still require repeated-seed checks; robustness can cost evaluations.

Integration: prospective: no PyAutoFit search wrapper found

Benchmark evidence: not_assessed: this literature review establishes no benchmark result

Investigation prompt
pocoMC

flow-preconditioned Monte Carlo · reviewed 2026-10-08

Paper ↗ · Official code ↗

Expected uses

  • Posterior and evidence investigation for strongly correlated nonlinear targets.

Limitations

  • Flow training, population coverage and evidence uncertainty need validation; check the pinned implementation against the 2022 method.

Integration: prospective: no PyAutoFit search wrapper found

Benchmark evidence: not_assessed: this literature review establishes no benchmark result
